    ---
    name: python-best-practices
    description: Python best practices with the pytest, ruff, and mypy commands and the Windows exe build. Use when reading or editing any .py file or pyproject.toml.
    paths: ["**/*.py", "**/pyproject.toml"]
    ---

    # Python best practices

    Apply the **type-system-discipline** principle skill first.

    ## Build and test

    Work from the project root, with the project's own interpreter. Run the commands one at a time from the folder named above and stop at the first non-zero exit. They are the gate for the Commit and push playbook. A project's own `CLAUDE.md` wins when it names different commands.

    ```text
    python -m pytest
    ```

    Use the interpreter the project already has: `.venv/Scripts/python.exe` when a `.venv` folder exists, otherwise the `python` on `PATH`. Do not create a virtual environment or install packages to satisfy the gate. When a test needs a package that is not installed, stop and report it.

    Add a line only when the project is set up for it:

    | Extra command | Add it when |
    |---|---|
    | `python -m ruff check .` | `pyproject.toml` has a `[tool.ruff]` table, or a `ruff.toml` exists. |
    | `python -m mypy .` | `pyproject.toml` has a `[tool.mypy]` table, or a `mypy.ini` exists. |

    | Project state | Change to the gate |
    |---|---|
    | No tests at all | The gate is one run of the real entry point with a real input. Say so. |
    | Tests use `unittest` | `python -m unittest discover tests`. |
    | The project ships a Windows exe | After the tests pass, `pyinstaller --onefile --console <entry>.py`, then run `dist\<entry>.exe --help` once. On Windows the `--add-data` separator is `;`. A missing module at run time takes `--hidden-import=<module>`. |
    | The user asks you to set the project up | `python -m venv .venv`, then `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt` or `-e .` for a `pyproject.toml` project. Call the interpreter by path. Activation scripts differ between shells. |

    ## Rules

    | Rule | Summary |
    |------|---------|
    | Type hints on every signature | Annotate parameters and returns. `mypy` or `pyright` checks them. A function without hints is unchecked. |
    | Closed variants | Model variants as frozen dataclasses in a `Union`, matched with `match` and `assert_never` in the default arm. |
    | Strong ids | `NewType` for ids and units, made by one parsing function at the boundary. |
    | Immutable data | `@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)` for values. `tuple` and `Mapping` in signatures. |
    | Parse at the boundary | Turn CLI args, JSON, CSV, and environment values into typed objects once. Inside, trust the type. See the **boundary-discipline** principle skill. |
    | No `Any` | External data is `object` until parsed. `Any` switches the checker off for everything it touches. |
    | Exceptions | Raise a specific exception. Catch a specific exception. Never a bare `except:`, and never `except Exception: pass`. |
    | Resources | `with` for every file, lock, connection, and temporary folder. |
    | Paths | `pathlib.Path`, never string joins. No hard-coded separators and no absolute user paths. |
    | Text encoding | Pass `encoding="utf-8"` to every `open`, `read_text`, and `write_text`. The Windows default is the ANSI code page, which corrupts Korean text. |
    | Bundled data | Code that ships as a PyInstaller exe finds its data through `sys._MEIPASS` when it is set, and the source folder otherwise. |
    | Subprocesses | `subprocess.run([...], check=True)` with an argument list. Never `shell=True` with text built from input. |
    | Real tests | `pytest` with `tmp_path` and real files. Parametrize the cases. Mock only what you cannot run locally. |
    | Logging | The `logging` module with a named logger. No `print` in library code. |

    Examples: `references/patterns.md`.
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:skills/python-best-practices/references/patterns.md`

<!-- BEGIN FILE: skills/python-best-practices/references/patterns.md -->
    # Python patterns

    Code examples for the rules in `SKILL.md`. See the **type-system-discipline** and **boundary-discipline** principle skills.

    ## Closed variants

    ```python
    from dataclasses import dataclass
    from typing import assert_never


    @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    class Loading:
        pass


    @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    class Ready:
        rows: tuple[str, ...]


    @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    class Failed:
        error: str


    LoadState = Loading | Ready | Failed


    def describe(state: LoadState) -> str:
        match state:
            case Loading():
                return "loading"
            case Ready(rows=rows):
                return f"{len(rows)} rows"
            case Failed(error=error):
                return error
            case _:
                assert_never(state)
    ```

    `match` and `slots=True` need Python 3.10, and `assert_never` needs 3.11. On 3.10 import `assert_never` from `typing_extensions`.

    ## Strong ids

    ```python
    from typing import NewType

    ElementId = NewType("ElementId", str)


    def parse_element_id(raw: str) -> ElementId:
        if not raw.startswith("/"):
            raise ValueError(f"not an element name: {raw!r}")
        return ElementId(raw)
    ```

    ## Text encoding and paths

    ```python
    from pathlib import Path


    def read_macro(folder: Path, name: str) -> str:
        return (folder / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ```

    ## Bundled data

    ```python
    import sys
    from pathlib import Path


    def resource_path(relative: str) -> Path:
        base = Path(getattr(sys, "_MEIPASS", Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
        return base / relative
    ```

    ## Real tests

    ```python
    import pytest


    @pytest.mark.parametrize(("raw", "ok"), [("/PIPE-1", True), ("PIPE-1", False), ("", False)])
    def test_parse_element_id(raw: str, ok: bool) -> None:
        if ok:
            assert parse_element_id(raw) == raw
        else:
            with pytest.raises(ValueError):
                parse_element_id(raw)
    ```
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:skills/rust-best-practices/SKILL.md`

표준 cargo, clippy, rustfmt 관례.

<!-- BEGIN FILE: skills/rust-best-practices/SKILL.md -->
    ---
    name: rust-best-practices
    description: Rust best practices with the cargo build, test, clippy, and fmt commands. Use when reading or editing any .rs file or Cargo.toml.
    paths: ["**/*.rs", "**/Cargo.toml"]
    ---

    # Rust best practices

    Apply the **type-system-discipline** principle skill first.

    ## Build and test

    Work from the workspace root. Run the commands one at a time from the folder named above and stop at the first non-zero exit. They are the gate for the Commit and push playbook. A project's own `CLAUDE.md` wins when it names different commands.

    ```text
    cargo build
    cargo test
    ```

    Add a line only when the project is set up for it:

    | Extra command | Add it when |
    |---|---|
    | `cargo fmt --all -- --check` | The `rustfmt` component is installed (`cargo fmt --version` exits 0). |
    | `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` | The `clippy` component is installed (`cargo clippy --version` exits 0). |
    | `--all-features` on `clippy` and `test` | The crate's features can all be on together. |
    | `cargo build --release` | The change ships a binary. |

    | Project state | Change to the gate |
    |---|---|
    | A workspace with several crates | Add `--workspace` to `clippy`, `test`, and `build`. |
    | Features that cannot be on together | Drop `--all-features` and run the gate once per supported feature set. Name the sets in the report. |
    | The MSVC linker is missing | `link.exe not found` means the Visual Studio C++ build tools are not installed. Report it. Do not switch the toolchain to work around it. |
    | A crate with `unsafe` | Also run `cargo +nightly miri test` when Miri is installed, and say when it is not. |

    ## Rules

    | Rule | Summary |
    |------|---------|
    | Enums for variants | Model a closed set as an `enum` and `match` it without a `_` arm, so a new variant is a compile error. |
    | Newtypes | Wrap ids and units in a tuple struct with a private field and one constructor that validates. |
    | Parse, don't validate | Turn external data into a typed value once, with `TryFrom` or `FromStr`. Inside, trust the type. See the **boundary-discipline** principle skill. |
    | `Result` over panic | Return `Result<T, E>` for anything that can fail at run time. `unwrap` and `expect` belong in tests and in cases a comment-free invariant makes impossible. |
    | Error types | `thiserror` enums in libraries, `anyhow::Result` with `.context(...)` in binaries. Never `Box<dyn Error>` in a public library API. |
    | Borrow by default | Take `&str`, `&[T]`, and `&Path`. Take ownership only when the function keeps the value. A `.clone()` added to quiet the borrow checker is a design question, not a fix. |
    | No stringly state | No `bool` pairs or `String` tags for state. Use an enum that carries the data each state needs. |
    | Iterators | Prefer iterator chains to index loops. Indexing with `[i]` can panic, `.get(i)` cannot. |
    | `unsafe` | Avoid it. When required, keep it in one small module behind a safe API, with the invariant written as a `// SAFETY:` line, which is a comment the **no-comments** skill keeps. |
    | Concurrency | Share with `Arc` plus a channel or a `Mutex`. Never hold a `std::sync::Mutex` guard across `.await`. |
    | Windows paths | `std::path::PathBuf` and `Path::join`, never string concatenation. `std::fs::canonicalize` returns a `\\?\` path on Windows, so do not show it to users or pass it to other tools. |
    | Lints as the bar | Where `clippy` is installed, `-D warnings` is part of the gate. Fix the lint. An `#[allow(...)]` needs a reason a reader can check. |
    | Real tests | Unit tests beside the code in `#[cfg(test)]`, integration tests under `tests/` that use only the public API. |
    | Dependencies | Commit `Cargo.lock` for binaries. Add a crate only when the standard library cannot do the job in a few lines. |

    Examples: `references/patterns.md`.
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:skills/rust-best-practices/references/patterns.md`

<!-- BEGIN FILE: skills/rust-best-practices/references/patterns.md -->
    # Rust patterns

    Code examples for the rules in `SKILL.md`. See the **type-system-discipline** and **boundary-discipline** principle skills.

    ## Enums for variants

    ```rust
    enum LoadState {
        Loading,
        Ready { rows: Vec<String> },
        Failed { error: String },
    }

    fn describe(state: &LoadState) -> String {
        match state {
            LoadState::Loading => "loading".to_owned(),
            LoadState::Ready { rows } => format!("{} rows", rows.len()),
            LoadState::Failed { error } => error.clone(),
        }
    }
    ```

    ## Newtypes that parse

    ```rust
    use std::str::FromStr;

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
    pub struct ElementId(String);

    #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
    #[error("not an element name: {0:?}")]
    pub struct ElementIdError(String);

    impl FromStr for ElementId {
        type Err = ElementIdError;

        fn from_str(raw: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
            if raw.starts_with('/') {
                Ok(Self(raw.to_owned()))
            } else {
                Err(ElementIdError(raw.to_owned()))
            }
        }
    }
    ```

    ## Errors with context in a binary

    ```rust
    use anyhow::{Context, Result};
    use std::path::Path;

    fn read_settings(path: &Path) -> Result<Settings> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        toml::from_str(&text).with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))
    }
    ```

    ## Tests beside the code

    ```rust
    #[cfg(test)]
    mod tests {
        use super::*;

        #[test]
        fn rejects_a_name_without_a_slash() {
            assert!("PIPE-1".parse::<ElementId>().is_err());
        }
    }
    ```
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:skills/pml-best-practices/SKILL.md`

규칙마다 출처 열이 있다. 사용자의 e3d-standalone, e3d-launcher, pml-crypto 스킬에서 온 것과 `unverified`를 나눴다.

<!-- BEGIN FILE: skills/pml-best-practices/SKILL.md -->
    ---
    name: pml-best-practices
    description: AVEVA PML best practices for E3D and PDMS macros, functions, forms, and objects, with how to load and run them. Use when reading or editing any .pmlfnc, .pmlfrm, .pmlobj, .pmlmac, or .mac file.
    paths: ["**/*.pmlfnc", "**/*.pmlfrm", "**/*.pmlobj", "**/*.pmlmac", "**/*.mac"]
    ---

    # PML best practices

    Apply the **boundary-discipline** principle skill first. PML has no compiler and no test runner, so the only proof is a run inside E3D.

    Every rule below names its source. `launcher`, `standalone`, and `crypto` are the user's own E3D skills (`e3d-launcher`, `e3d-standalone`, `pml-crypto`), which were checked against a real installation. `unverified` means general PML practice that nobody has checked here: confirm it in E3D before you rely on it, and tell the user when a change depends on one.

    ## Load and run

    PML has no build, so a PML project has no gate that runs from a shell. Declare the gate in the project's `CLAUDE.md`:

    ```text
    dstack-gate: manual - load the changed file in E3D and run <entry point>
    ```

    The Commit and push playbook then asks the user to confirm that run before it pushes, and records `Verification: manual (<what was run>)`. Without E3D on this machine you cannot run the proof yourself. Say so, hand the user the exact lines to run, and wait for their confirmation.

    | Step | How | Source |
    |---|---|---|
    | Run a macro file | `$M /<full path to the macro>` | standalone |
    | Print a value while proving a change | `$P $!value` | standalone |
    | Query into a variable | `VAR !result <query>`, as in `VAR !result FLNN` | standalone |
    | Run PML from a C# host | `Command.CreateCommand("$M /<macro>")`, then `Run()`. Read `Result` on success and `Error.MessageText()` on failure | standalone |
    | Start a console session that runs a macro | `mon.exe PROD E3D init "<launch.init>" TTY <project> <user>/<password> /<mdb> <macro path>` | launcher |
    | Make project PML findable | Put the project's PML folder first on `PMLLIB`, before the install folder | standalone |
    | Rebuild the `PMLLIB` index after adding or renaming a file | `pml rehash all` | unverified |
    | Reload one form or object after editing it | `pml reload form !!<FormName>`, `pml reload object <OBJECTNAME>` | unverified |

    Keep install paths, project codes, and credentials in a configuration file or environment variables that stay out of the repository, never in a committed file (launcher).

    ## Rules

    | Rule | Summary | Source |
    |------|---------|--------|
    | File types | PML source lives in `.pmlfnc` (function), `.pmlfrm` (form), `.pmlobj` (object), and `.pmlmac` (macro) files. Extension case does not matter. | crypto |
    | Published files are not source | A file whose first line is `--<004>-- Published PML 1.0`, or the `--<004-N>--` and `--<002>--` variants, is encrypted output. Edit the plain source and publish again. Never commit a hand-edited published file. | crypto |
    | Authorized code only | Decrypt or edit published PML only when it is the user's own code or code they are licensed to change. | crypto |
    | File encoding | Write generated macro files as UTF-8, and read PML back as UTF-8. Korean comments break when a tool re-reads them in the ANSI code page. After any round trip, check one Korean string and treat U+FFFD or `?` in its place as a failure. | standalone, crypto |
    | No silent continue | `onerror continue` belongs only in a generated batch macro whose host reads every result back. In hand-written PML it hides the first failure. | standalone |
    | Check the host's result | A C# host checks the return of `Run()` and logs `Error.MessageText()`. A macro that printed nothing is not a pass. | standalone |
    | x86 hosts | A C# host that loads AVEVA assemblies targets x86 and resolves them from the install folder. See the **csharp-best-practices** skill. | standalone |
    | No secrets in macros | A macro or launcher argument that carries a user name and password is built at run time from configuration. It is never written into a tracked file. | launcher |
    | Prove it in the session | After every edit, load the file, run the entry point once, and paste the command-window output into the report. | standalone |
    | One definition per file | A function, form, or object lives in a file named after it in lower case, because `PMLLIB` finds a definition by file name. | unverified |
    | Local before global | `!name` is local and `!!name` is global to the session. Use a global only for a form or a shared object. | unverified |
    | Typed members and arguments | Declare member and argument types (`is STRING`, `is REAL`). | unverified |
    | Handle the error you expect | Follow a command that can fail with a `handle` block for that one error. A catch-all handler hides every failure. | unverified |
    | Forms hold no rules | A form's methods read gadgets and call an object or function. | unverified |

    Syntax differs between PDMS 12 and E3D 2 and 3. For an `unverified` row, read the AVEVA PML reference for the installed version, or run a two-line probe in the command window, before writing code that depends on it.

    Examples: `references/patterns.md`.
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:skills/pml-best-practices/references/patterns.md`

출처 표시가 있는 예제. `unverified` 예제는 W08로 확인.

<!-- BEGIN FILE: skills/pml-best-practices/references/patterns.md -->
    # PML patterns

    Examples for the rules in `SKILL.md`. Each one names its source the same way: `standalone` and `launcher` come from the user's checked E3D skills, and `unverified` examples have not been run. Confirm an `unverified` example in E3D before copying it.

    ## A generated macro and its host (standalone)

    The host writes the macro as UTF-8, runs it, and reads the result back.

    ```
    onerror continue
    VAR !result FLNN
    $P $!result
    ```

    ```csharp
    File.WriteAllText(macroPath, macroText, Encoding.UTF8);
    Command command = Command.CreateCommand("$M /" + macroPath);
    if (!command.Run())
    {
        throw new InvalidOperationException(command.Error.MessageText());
    }
    string output = command.Result.Trim();
    ```

    `onerror continue` is acceptable here only because the host checks `Run()` and reads the output.

    ## Starting a console session with a macro (launcher)

    ```text
    mon.exe PROD E3D init "<install folder>\launch.init" TTY <PROJECT> <user>/<password> /<MDB> <macro path>
    ```

    Build this line at run time from a configuration file that is not tracked. `TTY` runs without a window, `CONSOLE` shows one for debugging.

    ## A function in its own file (unverified)

    ```
    define function !!projPipeLength(!pipe is DBREF) is REAL
        !total = 0
        do !branch values !pipe.members
            !total = !total + 1
        enddo
        return !total
    endfunction
    ```

    ## Handling one expected error (unverified)

    ```
    !file = object FILE(!path)
    !file.open('READ')
    handle ANY
        $P cannot open $!path
        return
    endhandle
    ```

    Replace the catch-all with the module and number of the one error you expect. Read both from the command window the first time the failure happens.
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:scripts/check-dstack.mjs`

이름과 링크 검사는 원본 생성기 `generate-pstack-harness-trees.mjs`의 `SKILL_NAME`, `LINK`, `resolve` 로직을 따랐다.

<!-- BEGIN FILE: scripts/check-dstack.mjs -->
    #!/usr/bin/env node
    // dstack repository check. Zero dependencies. Needs Node 22.18 or later, on Windows or macOS. Development tool: the plugin does not load it.
    // Usage: node scripts/check-dstack.mjs [--root DIR] [--config FILE] [--only a,b] [--json] [--counts] [--no-validate] [--require-validate]
    // Exit codes: 0 no findings, 1 findings, 2 usage or configuration error.
    import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
    import { dirname, extname, join, posix, relative, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
    import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";

    const CHECKS = ["residue", "frontmatter", "links", "routes", "manifest", "ps1", "counts", "encoding", "language", "languageSkills", "required"];
    const [nodeMajor, nodeMinor] = process.versions.node.split(".").map(Number);
    if (nodeMajor < 22 || (nodeMajor === 22 && nodeMinor < 18)) {
      console.error("check-dstack: Node 22.18 or later is required, found " + process.versions.node + ". Install the current LTS from nodejs.org, then run this again.");
      process.exit(2);
    }
    const SKILL_NAME = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
    const LINK = /\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g;

    function fail(message) {
      console.error("check-dstack: " + message);
      process.exit(2);
    }

    function parseArgs(argv) {
      const args = { root: undefined, config: undefined, only: CHECKS, json: false, counts: false, validate: "auto" };
      for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
        const a = argv[i];
        const value = () => (i + 1 < argv.length ? argv[++i] : fail(a + " needs a value"));
        if (a === "--root") args.root = value();
        else if (a === "--config") args.config = value();
        else if (a === "--only") args.only = value().split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        else if (a === "--json") args.json = true;
        else if (a === "--counts") args.counts = true;
        else if (a === "--no-validate") args.validate = "never";
        else if (a === "--require-validate") args.validate = "require";
        else fail("unknown argument: " + a);
      }
      for (const name of args.only) if (!CHECKS.includes(name)) fail("unknown check: " + name + " (known: " + CHECKS.join(", ") + ")");
      return args;
    }

    const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
    const ROOT = resolve(args.root ?? join(here, ".."));
    const CONFIG_PATH = resolve(args.config ?? process.env.DSTACK_CHECK_CONFIG ?? join(here, "check-dstack.config.json"));
    if (!existsSync(ROOT) || !statSync(ROOT).isDirectory()) fail("root is not a directory: " + ROOT);
    if (!existsSync(CONFIG_PATH)) fail("config not found: " + CONFIG_PATH);
    let config;
    try {
      config = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CONFIG_PATH, "utf8"));
    } catch (error) {
      fail("config is not valid JSON: " + error.message);
    }
    for (const key of ["version", "expected", "scan", "allowlist", "residue", "routes", "ps1", "language", "languageSkills", "required"]) {
      if (!(key in config)) fail("config is missing key: " + key);
    }
    if (config.version !== 1) fail("unsupported config version: " + config.version);

    const findings = [];
    const notes = [];
    const add = (check, rule, path, line, message) => findings.push({ check, rule, path, line, message });

    function globToRegExp(glob) {
      const escaped = glob.replace(/[.+^$()|[\]{}\\]/g, "\\$&").replace(/\*\*/g, "\u0000").replace(/\*/g, "[^/]*").replace(/\u0000/g, ".*");
      return new RegExp("^" + escaped + "$");
    }
    const allow = config.allowlist.map((entry) => {
      if (!entry.path || !Array.isArray(entry.rules) || !entry.reason) fail("allowlist entry needs path, rules, reason: " + JSON.stringify(entry));
      return { ...entry, re: globToRegExp(entry.path) };
    });
    function allowed(path, rule, line) {
      return allow.some((e) => e.re.test(path) && (e.rules.includes("*") || e.rules.includes(rule)) && (e.line === undefined || e.line === line));
    }

    function walk(dir) {
      return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
        if (config.scan.excludeDirs.includes(name) || name === ".DS_Store") return [];
        const path = join(dir, name);
        return statSync(path).isDirectory() ? walk(path) : [path];
      });
    }
    const rel = (path) => relative(ROOT, path).split(sep).join("/");
    const all = walk(ROOT).map((abs) => ({ abs, path: rel(abs), ext: extname(abs).toLowerCase() }));
    const isBinary = (f) => config.scan.binaryExtensions.includes(f.ext) || readFileSync(f.abs).subarray(0, 8000).includes(0);
    const textFiles = all.filter((f) => !isBinary(f));
    const read = (f) => readFileSync(f.abs, "utf8");
    const exists = (path) => existsSync(join(ROOT, path));
    const run = (name) => args.only.includes(name);

    if (run("residue")) {
      const rules = config.residue.map((rule) => {
        try {
          return { ...rule, res: rule.patterns.map((p) => new RegExp(p, "g" + rule.flags)) };
        } catch (error) {
          fail("bad residue pattern in rule " + rule.id + ": " + error.message);
        }
      });
      for (const f of textFiles) {
        const prose = config.scan.proseExtensions.includes(f.ext);
        const lines = read(f).split(/\r?\n/);
        lines.forEach((text, index) => {
          const seen = new Set();
          for (const rule of rules) {
            if (rule.scope === "prose" && !prose) continue;
            if (rule.scope === "code" && prose) continue;
            if (allowed(f.path, rule.id, index + 1)) continue;
            for (const re of rule.res) {
              re.lastIndex = 0;
              for (const m of text.matchAll(re)) {
                const key = rule.id + ":" + m.index;
                if (seen.has(key)) continue;
                seen.add(key);
                add("residue", rule.id, f.path, index + 1, JSON.stringify(m[0]) + " in: " + text.trim().slice(0, 120));
              }
            }
          }
        });
      }
    }

    function frontmatter(text) {
      const block = text.match(/^---\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n---/)?.[1];
      if (block === undefined) return undefined;
      const field = (key) => block.match(new RegExp("^" + key + ":(.*)$", "m"))?.[1].trim().replace(/^(["'])([\s\S]*)\1$/, "$2");
      return { name: field("name"), description: field("description"), block };
    }
    const skillsDir = join(ROOT, "skills");
    const skillNames = existsSync(skillsDir) ? readdirSync(skillsDir).filter((n) => statSync(join(skillsDir, n)).isDirectory()) : [];
    const agentsDir = join(ROOT, "agents");
    const agentFiles = existsSync(agentsDir) ? readdirSync(agentsDir).filter((n) => n.endsWith(".md")) : [];

    if (run("frontmatter")) {
      for (const skill of skillNames) {
        const path = "skills/" + skill + "/SKILL.md";
        if (!exists(path)) { add("frontmatter", "SKILL_MISSING", path, 0, "skill folder has no SKILL.md"); continue; }
        const fm = frontmatter(readFileSync(join(ROOT, path), "utf8"));
        if (!fm) { add("frontmatter", "NO_FRONTMATTER", path, 1, "no YAML frontmatter block"); continue; }
        if (fm.name !== skill) add("frontmatter", "NAME_MISMATCH", path, 1, "name " + JSON.stringify(fm.name ?? "") + " is not the folder name " + skill);
        if (!SKILL_NAME.test(skill) || skill.length > 64) add("frontmatter", "BAD_NAME", path, 1, "folder name must be lowercase words joined by hyphens, at most 64 characters");
        if (!fm.description) add("frontmatter", "NO_DESCRIPTION", path, 1, "description is missing or empty");
      }
      for (const file of agentFiles) {
        const path = "agents/" + file;
        const fm = frontmatter(readFileSync(join(ROOT, path), "utf8"));
        if (!fm) { add("frontmatter", "NO_FRONTMATTER", path, 1, "no YAML frontmatter block"); continue; }
        if (fm.name !== file.slice(0, -3)) add("frontmatter", "NAME_MISMATCH", path, 1, "name " + JSON.stringify(fm.name ?? "") + " is not the file name " + file.slice(0, -3));
        if (!fm.description) add("frontmatter", "NO_DESCRIPTION", path, 1, "description is missing or empty");
      }
    }

    const mdFiles = textFiles.filter((f) => f.ext === ".md" && !allowed(f.path, "LINKS", 0));
    if (run("links")) {
      for (const f of mdFiles) {
        read(f).split(/\r?\n/).forEach((text, index) => {
          for (const [, href] of text.matchAll(LINK)) {
            const target = href.replace(/[#?].*/, "");
            if (!target || /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(target) || target.includes("<")) continue;
            const resolved = posix.normalize(posix.join(posix.dirname(f.path), target)).replace(/\/$/, "");
            if (resolved.startsWith("..")) add("links", "LINK_ESCAPES", f.path, index + 1, href + " leaves the repository");
            else if (!exists(resolved)) add("links", "LINK_BROKEN", f.path, index + 1, href + " does not exist (resolved to " + resolved + ")");
          }
        });
      }
    }

    const kebab = (title) => title.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
    if (run("routes")) {
      const dMode = "skills/" + config.expected.dModeSkill;
      const playbookDir = join(ROOT, dMode, "playbooks");
      const onDisk = existsSync(playbookDir) ? readdirSync(playbookDir).filter((n) => n.endsWith(".md")).map((n) => n.slice(0, -3)) : [];
      if (!exists(dMode + "/SKILL.md")) add("routes", "ROUTER_MISSING", dMode + "/SKILL.md", 0, "router skill not found");
      else {
        const text = readFileSync(join(ROOT, dMode, "SKILL.md"), "utf8");
        const section = text.split(/^## Playbooks\s*$/m)[1]?.split(/^## /m)[0] ?? "";
        const listLine = section.split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => l.trim()).pop() ?? "";
        const listed = listLine.replace(/\.\s*$/, "").split(",").map(kebab).filter(Boolean);
        for (const name of listed) if (!onDisk.includes(name)) add("routes", "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_PLAYBOOK", dMode + "/SKILL.md", 0, "Playbooks list names " + name + " but playbooks/" + name + ".md does not exist");
        for (const name of onDisk) if (!listed.includes(name)) add("routes", "PLAYBOOK_NOT_ROUTED", dMode + "/playbooks/" + name + ".md", 0, "playbook file is not in the Playbooks list");
      }
      const external = new Set(config.routes.externalCommands);
      const ignore = new RegExp(config.routes.ignoreCommandPattern);
      const known = new Set(skillNames);
      for (const f of mdFiles) {
        read(f).split(/\r?\n/).forEach((text, index) => {
          for (const m of text.matchAll(/playbooks\/([a-z0-9-]+)\.md/g)) {
            if (!onDisk.includes(m[1])) add("routes", "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_PLAYBOOK", f.path, index + 1, "references playbooks/" + m[1] + ".md, which does not exist");
          }
          for (const m of text.matchAll(/the \*\*([a-z0-9-]+)\*\* (principle )?skill/g)) {
            const name = m[2] && !m[1].startsWith("principle-") ? "principle-" + m[1] : m[1];
            if (!known.has(name)) add("routes", "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_SKILL", f.path, index + 1, "names the " + m[1] + " skill, but skills/" + name + " does not exist");
          }
          for (const m of text.matchAll(/\*\*(principle-[a-z0-9-]+)\*\*/g)) {
            if (!known.has(m[1])) add("routes", "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_SKILL", f.path, index + 1, "names " + m[1] + ", but skills/" + m[1] + " does not exist");
          }
          for (const m of text.matchAll(/(?<![\w/.~:-])\/(?:dstack:)?([a-z][a-z0-9-]*)(?![\w/.<-])/g)) {
            const name = m[1];
            if (known.has(name) || external.has(name) || ignore.test(name)) continue;
            const before = text.slice(0, m.index);
            const inCode = (before.match(/`/g) ?? []).length % 2 === 1;
            if (inCode && m[0].includes("dstack:")) add("routes", "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_SKILL", f.path, index + 1, "command " + m[0] + " names no skill in skills/");
          }
        });
      }
    }

    if (run("manifest")) {
      const readJson = (path) => {
        if (!exists(path)) { add("manifest", "MANIFEST_MISSING", path, 0, "file not found"); return undefined; }
        try { return JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, path), "utf8")); }
        catch (error) { add("manifest", "MANIFEST_INVALID_JSON", path, 0, error.message); return undefined; }
      };
      const plugin = readJson(".claude-plugin/plugin.json");
      const market = readJson(".claude-plugin/marketplace.json");
      if (plugin) {
        if (plugin.name !== config.expected.pluginName) add("manifest", "PLUGIN_NAME", ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", 0, "name is " + JSON.stringify(plugin.name) + ", expected " + config.expected.pluginName);
        if ("version" in plugin) add("manifest", "PLUGIN_VERSION_PINNED", ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", 0, "remove version: the commit is the version, and a pinned version hides later commits from plugin updates");
        for (const key of ["description", "license"]) if (!plugin[key]) add("manifest", "PLUGIN_FIELD", ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", 0, "missing " + key);
      }
      if (market) {
        if (market.name !== config.expected.marketplaceName) add("manifest", "MARKETPLACE_NAME", ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", 0, "name is " + JSON.stringify(market.name) + ", expected " + config.expected.marketplaceName);
        const entry = Array.isArray(market.plugins) ? market.plugins.find((p) => p.name === config.expected.pluginName) : undefined;
        if (!entry) add("manifest", "MARKETPLACE_ENTRY", ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", 0, "no plugins[] entry named " + config.expected.pluginName);
        else if (entry.source !== "./") add("manifest", "MARKETPLACE_SOURCE", ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", 0, "source must be \"./\"");
        if (entry && "version" in entry) add("manifest", "PLUGIN_VERSION_PINNED", ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", 0, "remove version from the plugins[] entry");
        if (!market.description && !market.metadata?.description) add("manifest", "MARKETPLACE_DESCRIPTION", ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", 0, "missing description");
      }
      if (args.validate !== "never") {
        const bin = process.env.DSTACK_CHECK_CLAUDE_BIN ?? "claude";
        const r = spawnSync(bin, ["plugin", "validate", ROOT], { encoding: "utf8", shell: process.platform === "win32" });
        if (r.error || r.status === null) {
          if (args.validate === "require") add("manifest", "VALIDATE_UNAVAILABLE", ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", 0, "could not run " + bin + " plugin validate");
          else notes.push("SKIP claude plugin validate: " + bin + " is not runnable here");
        } else if (r.status !== 0) {
          add("manifest", "VALIDATE_FAILED", ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", 0, "claude plugin validate exited " + r.status + ": " + (r.stdout + r.stderr).split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => /✘|error/i.test(l)).slice(0, 5).join(" | "));
        } else notes.push("OK claude plugin validate exited 0");
      }
    }

    // Walks a PowerShell source once and reports the first unclosed string, comment, here-string, or bracket.
    function ps1Balance(text) {
      const stack = [];
      const pairs = { ")": "(", "}": "{", "]": "[" };
      let line = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
        const ch = text[i];
        const next = text[i + 1];
        if (ch === "\n") { line++; continue; }
        if (ch === "<" && next === "#") {
          const end = text.indexOf("#>", i + 2);
          if (end === -1) return { line, message: "block comment is never closed" };
          line += text.slice(i, end).split("\n").length - 1;
          i = end + 1;
        } else if (ch === "#") {
          while (i + 1 < text.length && text[i + 1] !== "\n") i++;
        } else if (ch === "@" && (next === "'" || next === '"')) {
          const end = text.indexOf("\n" + next + "@", i + 2);
          if (end === -1) return { line, message: "here-string is never closed" };
          line += text.slice(i, end + 1).split("\n").length - 1;
          i = end + 2;
        } else if (ch === "'" || ch === '"') {
          const start = line;
          let j = i + 1;
          for (; j < text.length; j++) {
            if (text[j] === "\n") line++;
            if (ch === '"' && text[j] === String.fromCharCode(96)) { j++; continue; }
            if (text[j] === ch) { if (text[j + 1] === ch) { j++; continue; } break; }
          }
          if (j >= text.length) return { line: start, message: "string is never closed" };
          i = j;
        } else if (ch === "(" || ch === "{" || ch === "[") {
          stack.push({ ch, line });
        } else if (ch in pairs) {
          const open = stack.pop();
          if (!open || open.ch !== pairs[ch]) return { line, message: "unexpected " + ch };
        }
      }
      const open = stack.pop();
      return open ? { line: open.line, message: open.ch + " is never closed" } : undefined;
    }

    if (run("ps1")) {
      const compile = (list) => list.map((d) => ({ ...d, re: new RegExp(d.pattern) }));
      const deny = compile(config.ps1.denylist);
      const fenceDeny = compile(config.ps1.fenceDenylist);
      const stripped = (line) => line.replace(/'[^']*'/g, "''").replace(/"[^"]*"/g, '""').replace(/#.*$/, "");
      for (const f of all.filter((f) => f.ext === ".ps1")) {
        const bytes = readFileSync(f.abs);
        if (config.ps1.requireAscii) {
          const index = bytes.findIndex((b) => b > 0x7e || (b < 0x20 && b !== 0x0a && b !== 0x0d && b !== 0x09));
          if (index !== -1) add("ps1", "PS1_NON_ASCII", f.path, bytes.subarray(0, index).toString("latin1").split("\n").length, "byte 0x" + bytes[index].toString(16) + " is outside printable ASCII");
        }
        const text = bytes.toString("latin1");
        if (config.ps1.requireCrlf && /(^|[^\r])\n/.test(text)) add("ps1", "PS1_LF_ONLY", f.path, 0, "line endings must be CRLF");
        const unbalanced = ps1Balance(text);
        if (unbalanced) add("ps1", "PS1_UNBALANCED", f.path, unbalanced.line, unbalanced.message);
        const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
        for (const required of config.ps1.requiredLines) {
          if (!lines.some((l) => new RegExp(required).test(l))) add("ps1", "PS1_REQUIRED_LINE", f.path, 0, "missing line matching " + required);
        }
        lines.forEach((line, index) => {
          if (/^\s*#/.test(line)) return;
          const code = stripped(line);
          for (const d of deny) if (d.re.test(code)) add("ps1", "PS1_DENYLIST", f.path, index + 1, d.why + " in: " + line.trim().slice(0, 100));
        });
      }
      for (const f of mdFiles) {
        let inFence = false;
        read(f).split(/\r?\n/).forEach((line, index) => {
          if (/^\s*```/.test(line)) { inFence = !inFence && /^\s*```(powershell|ps1|pwsh)\b/i.test(line); return; }
          if (!inFence) return;
          for (const d of fenceDeny) if (d.re.test(line)) add("ps1", "PS1_FENCE_DENYLIST", f.path, index + 1, d.why + " in: " + line.trim().slice(0, 100));
        });
      }
    }

    const counts = {
      skills: skillNames.filter((n) => exists("skills/" + n + "/SKILL.md")).length,
      principleSkills: skillNames.filter((n) => n.startsWith("principle-")).length,
      agents: agentFiles.length,
      playbooks: existsSync(join(ROOT, "skills", config.expected.dModeSkill, "playbooks")) ? readdirSync(join(ROOT, "skills", config.expected.dModeSkill, "playbooks")).filter((n) => n.endsWith(".md")).length : 0,
      guidePages: all.filter((f) => /^docs\/guide\/[^/]+\.md$/.test(f.path)).length,
      files: all.length,
    };
    if (run("counts")) {
      for (const key of ["skills", "principleSkills", "agents", "playbooks", "guidePages"]) {
        if (counts[key] !== config.expected[key]) add("counts", "COUNT_" + key.toUpperCase(), ".", 0, key + " is " + counts[key] + ", expected " + config.expected[key]);
      }
    }

    if (run("encoding")) {
      const decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true });
      for (const f of textFiles) {
        const bytes = readFileSync(f.abs);
        if (bytes[0] === 0xef && bytes[1] === 0xbb && bytes[2] === 0xbf) add("encoding", "BOM", f.path, 1, "file starts with a UTF-8 byte order mark");
        try { decoder.decode(bytes); } catch { add("encoding", "NOT_UTF8", f.path, 0, "file is not valid UTF-8"); }
        if (f.ext !== ".ps1" && f.ext !== ".bat" && f.ext !== ".cmd" && !allowed(f.path, "CRLF", 0) && bytes.includes(0x0d)) add("encoding", "CRLF", f.path, 0, "carriage return found; only .ps1 files use CRLF");
      }
    }

    if (run("language")) {
      const korean = config.language.koreanRequired.map(globToRegExp);
      const english = config.language.englishOnly.map(globToRegExp);
      const hangul = /[\uAC00-\uD7A3]/g;
      for (const f of textFiles) {
        const text = read(f);
        const count = (text.match(hangul) ?? []).length;
        if (korean.some((re) => re.test(f.path)) && count < config.language.minHangul) add("language", "KOREAN_MISSING", f.path, 0, "has " + count + " Hangul characters, needs at least " + config.language.minHangul);
        if (english.some((re) => re.test(f.path)) && count > 0 && !allowed(f.path, "HANGUL_IN_SKILL", 0)) add("language", "HANGUL_IN_SKILL", f.path, text.split(/\r?\n/).findIndex((l) => /[\uAC00-\uD7A3]/.test(l)) + 1, "skill and agent files are written in English");
      }
    }

    if (run("languageSkills")) {
      const spec = config.languageSkills;
      const router = exists("skills/" + config.expected.dModeSkill + "/SKILL.md") ? readFileSync(join(ROOT, "skills", config.expected.dModeSkill, "SKILL.md"), "utf8") : "";
      for (const name of spec.names) {
        const path = "skills/" + name + "/SKILL.md";
        if (!exists(path)) { add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_MISSING", path, 0, "language skill not found"); continue; }
        const text = readFileSync(join(ROOT, path), "utf8");
        const fm = frontmatter(text)?.block ?? "";
        if (!/^paths: \[.+\]\s*$/m.test(fm)) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_FRONTMATTER", path, 1, "frontmatter needs a paths list so the skill loads when a matching file is opened");
        if (/^disable-model-invocation:\s*true\s*$/m.test(fm)) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_FRONTMATTER", path, 1, "disable-model-invocation: true would stop paths from loading the skill");
        const headings = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => l.startsWith("## "));
        if (!spec.gateHeadings.some((h) => headings.includes(h))) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_SECTION", path, 0, "needs one of: " + spec.gateHeadings.join(", "));
        for (const h of spec.requiredHeadings) if (!headings.includes(h)) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_SECTION", path, 0, "needs the heading " + h);
        if (!exists("skills/" + name + "/" + spec.patternsFile)) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_PATTERNS", "skills/" + name + "/" + spec.patternsFile, 0, "examples file not found");
        if (!router.includes("**" + name + "**")) add("languageSkills", "LANG_SKILL_NOT_ROUTED", "skills/" + config.expected.dModeSkill + "/SKILL.md", 0, "the router does not name **" + name + "**");
      }
    }

    if (run("required")) {
      for (const entry of config.required) {
        if (!entry.path || !Array.isArray(entry.contains) || !entry.reason) fail("required entry needs path, contains, reason: " + JSON.stringify(entry).slice(0, 120));
        if (!exists(entry.path)) { add("required", "REQUIRED_FILE_MISSING", entry.path, 0, entry.reason); continue; }
        const text = readFileSync(join(ROOT, entry.path), "utf8");
        for (const needle of entry.contains) if (!text.includes(needle)) add("required", "REQUIRED_TEXT_MISSING", entry.path, 0, JSON.stringify(needle) + " is missing (" + entry.reason + ")");
        for (const needle of entry.absent ?? []) if (text.includes(needle)) add("required", "FORBIDDEN_TEXT", entry.path, text.split(/\r?\n/).findIndex((l) => l.includes(needle)) + 1, JSON.stringify(needle) + " must not appear (" + entry.reason + ")");
      }
    }

    const byRule = {};
    for (const f of findings) {
      byRule[f.rule] ??= { hits: 0, files: new Set() };
      byRule[f.rule].hits++;
      byRule[f.rule].files.add(f.path);
    }
    const summary = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byRule).map(([rule, v]) => [rule, { hits: v.hits, files: v.files.size }]));
    if (args.json) {
      console.log(JSON.stringify({ root: ROOT, counts, summary, notes, findings }, null, 2));
    } else {
      if (!args.counts) for (const f of findings) console.log("FAIL [" + f.check + "/" + f.rule + "] " + f.path + (f.line ? ":" + f.line : "") + " " + f.message);
      for (const note of notes) console.log(note);
      console.log("counts: " + JSON.stringify(counts));
      for (const [rule, v] of Object.entries(summary)) console.log("summary: " + rule + " " + v.hits + " hits in " + v.files + " files");
      console.log(findings.length === 0 ? "PASS check-dstack: 0 findings" : "FAIL check-dstack: " + findings.length + " findings");
    }
    process.exit(findings.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);
<!-- END FILE -->

#### `NEW:scripts/check-dstack.config.json`

검사 규칙, 허용 목록, 기대 개수.

<!-- BEGIN FILE: scripts/check-dstack.config.json -->
    {
      "$comment": "dstack repository check configuration. All patterns are JavaScript RegExp sources. Edit this file, not check-dstack.mjs, to change rules.",
      "version": 1,
      "expected": {
        "pluginName": "dstack",
        "marketplaceName": "d-stack",
        "skills": 62,
        "playbooks": 19,
        "agents": 2,
        "principleSkills": 24,
        "dModeSkill": "d-mode",
        "guidePages": 11
      },
      "scan": {
        "excludeDirs": [
          ".git",
          "node_modules",
          ".omc"
        ],
        "binaryExtensions": [
          ".png",
          ".jpg",
          ".jpeg",
          ".gif",
          ".ico",
          ".exe",
          ".dll",
          ".pdf",
          ".zip",
          ".mp4"
        ],
        "proseExtensions": [

          ".txt"
        ]
      },
      "allowlist": [
        {
          "path": "LICENSE",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "MIT notice must keep the upstream copyright holder"
        },
        {

          "rules": [

          ],

          ],
          "reason": "dh-dev plan archive, not plugin content"
        },

          "rules": [

          ],
          "reason": "dh-dev plan index"
        },
        {
          "path": "docs/revisions/**",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "revision-tracker logs"
        },
        {
          "path": "docs/revision_history.md",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "revision-tracker index"
        },
        {
          "path": "scripts/check-dstack.config.json",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "this file defines the banned terms"
        },
        {
          "path": "tests/**",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "adversarial fixtures plant banned terms on purpose"
        },
        {
          "path": "skills/statechart/scripts/statechart",
          "rules": [
            "SHELL"
          ],
          "reason": "upstream POSIX launcher that test/cli.test.ts exercises; Windows uses the node command in SKILL.md"
        },
        {
          "path": "skills/statechart/scripts/test/**",
          "rules": [
            "SHELL",
            "CRLF",
            "IDENTITY"
          ],
          "reason": "upstream test fixtures and recorded TLC output are kept byte for byte, so they keep the recording machine's paths, which include the upstream folder name"
        },
        {
          "path": "scripts/migration/**",
          "rules": [
            "*"
          ],
          "reason": "one-time migration record that maps upstream names to dstack names, so it holds every upstream term"
        }
      ],
      "residue": [
        {
          "id": "PR",
          "scope": "all",
          "flags": "",
          "patterns": [
            "\\bPRs?\\b",
            "\\bgh pr\\b",
            "\\borigin pr\\b"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "PR",
          "scope": "all",
          "flags": "i",
          "patterns": [
            "pull[ -]requests?",
            "merge queue",
            "merge-ready",
            "auto-merge",
            "stacked (prs?|diffs?)",
            "\\bGraphite\\b"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "PR",
          "scope": "prose",
          "flags": "i",
          "patterns": [
            "\\bprs?\\b"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "CURSOR",
          "scope": "prose",
          "flags": "i",
          "patterns": [
            "cursor"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "CURSOR",
          "scope": "code",
          "flags": "",
          "patterns": [
            "\\bCursor\\b",
            "\\.cursor\\b",
            "@cursor\\b",
            "cursor[-.](team-kit|skill|plugin|sh|com)",
            "cursor/plugins"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "CURSOR",
          "scope": "all",
          "flags": "i",
          "patterns": [
            "bugbot",
            "\\.mdc\\b",
            "deslop",
            "control-ui",
            "control-cli",
            "/add-plugin",
            "custom modes?",
            "/in-cloud",
            "cloud (sub)?agents?",
            "agent-transcripts",
            "agents window",
            "create-skill",
            "control skill",
            "project workers?"
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "HARNESS",
          "scope": "all",
          "flags": "",
          "patterns": [

#### `NEW:scripts/migration/port-from-upstream.mjs`

일회용 이식 도구(D-18).

<!-- BEGIN FILE: scripts/migration/port-from-upstream.mjs -->
    #!/usr/bin/env node
    // One-time migration tool. It ported the pinned upstream commit named in port-map.json into this tree: it copies
    // files, applies the line edits and literal replacements in port-map.json, and skips deleted and hand-written paths.
    // The edits are keyed to line numbers of that one commit, so this is a record of the port, not a resync path.
    // docs/UPSTREAM.md describes how to review a newer upstream by hand.
    // Usage: node scripts/migration/port-from-upstream.mjs --upstream DIR --out DIR [--map FILE] [--dry-run]
    // Exit codes: 0 done, 1 an edit anchor no longer matches upstream (the upstream line changed), 2 usage error.
    import { chmodSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    import { dirname, join, relative, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
    import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

    const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
    const opt = (name) => (argv.includes(name) ? argv[argv.indexOf(name) + 1] : undefined);
    const usage = (message) => { console.error("port-from-upstream: " + message); process.exit(2); };
    const UP = opt("--upstream") ? resolve(opt("--upstream")) : usage("--upstream DIR is required");
    const OUT = opt("--out") ? resolve(opt("--out")) : usage("--out DIR is required");
    const MAP = resolve(opt("--map") ?? join(here, "port-map.json"));
    const DRY = argv.includes("--dry-run");
    if (!existsSync(UP) || !statSync(UP).isDirectory()) usage("upstream is not a directory: " + UP);
    if (!existsSync(MAP)) usage("map not found: " + MAP);
    let map;
    try { map = JSON.parse(readFileSync(MAP, "utf8")); } catch (error) { usage("map is not valid JSON: " + error.message); }
    for (const key of ["version", "delete", "handWritten", "verbatim", "rename", "edits", "replace", "binaryExtensions"]) if (!(key in map)) usage("map is missing key: " + key);
    if (map.version !== 1) usage("unsupported map version: " + map.version);

    const glob = (g) => new RegExp("^" + g.replace(/[.+^$()|[\]{}\\]/g, "\\$&").replace(/\*\*/g, "\u0000").replace(/\*/g, "[^/]*").replace(/\u0000/g, ".*") + "$");
    const deleted = map.delete.map(glob);
    const handWritten = map.handWritten.map(glob);
    const verbatim = map.verbatim.map(glob);
    function walk(dir) {
      return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
        if (name === ".git" || name === "node_modules" || name === ".DS_Store") return [];
        const path = join(dir, name);
        return statSync(path).isDirectory() ? walk(path) : [path];
      });
    }
    function target(path) {
      for (const [from, to] of map.rename) if (path === from || path.startsWith(from)) return to + path.slice(from.length);
      return path;
    }
    const editsByPath = new Map();
    for (const e of map.edits) editsByPath.set(e.path, [...(editsByPath.get(e.path) ?? []), e]);

    const [nodeMajor, nodeMinor] = process.versions.node.split(".").map(Number);
    if (nodeMajor < 22 || (nodeMajor === 22 && nodeMinor < 18)) usage("Node 22.18 or later is required, found " + process.versions.node);
    const stats = { copied: 0, edited: 0, skippedDeleted: 0, skippedHandWritten: 0, replacements: 0 };
    const problems = [];
    const seenEditPaths = new Set();
    for (const abs of walk(UP)) {
      const path = relative(UP, abs).split(sep).join("/");
      if (deleted.some((re) => re.test(path))) { stats.skippedDeleted++; continue; }
      if (handWritten.some((re) => re.test(path))) { stats.skippedHandWritten++; continue; }
      const out = join(OUT, target(path));
      const bytes = readFileSync(abs);
      const binary = map.binaryExtensions.some((ext) => path.toLowerCase().endsWith(ext)) || bytes.subarray(0, 8000).includes(0);
      let result = bytes;
      if (!binary && !verbatim.some((re) => re.test(path))) {
        const lines = bytes.toString("utf8").split("\n");
        const drop = new Set();
        for (const e of editsByPath.get(path) ?? []) {
          seenEditPaths.add(path);
          const index = e.line - 1;
          if (index >= lines.length) { problems.push(path + ":" + e.line + " is past the end of the file"); continue; }
          if (e.old === "*") { if (e.new === null) drop.add(index); else lines[index] = e.new; }
          else if (!lines[index].includes(e.old)) problems.push(path + ":" + e.line + " no longer contains " + JSON.stringify(e.old.slice(0, 60)));
          else lines[index] = lines[index].replace(e.old, () => e.new);
          stats.edited++;
        }
        let text = lines.filter((_, i) => !drop.has(i)).join("\n");
        for (const rule of map.replace) {
          if (rule.only && !glob(rule.only).test(path)) continue;
          const parts = text.split(rule.from);
          stats.replacements += parts.length - 1;
          text = parts.join(rule.to);
        }
        result = Buffer.from(text, "utf8");
      }
      if (!DRY) {
        mkdirSync(dirname(out), { recursive: true });
        writeFileSync(out, result);
        chmodSync(out, statSync(abs).mode & 0o777);
      }
      stats.copied++;
    }
    for (const path of editsByPath.keys()) if (!seenEditPaths.has(path)) problems.push(path + " has edits in the map but was not copied (missing upstream, deleted, or hand-written)");
    for (const problem of problems) console.log("FAIL " + problem);
    console.log("port-from-upstream: " + JSON.stringify(stats) + (DRY ? " (dry run)" : ""));
    process.exit(problems.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);
<!-- END FILE -->

        {
          "path": "skills/show-me-your-work/SKILL.md",
          "line": 38,
          "old": "*",
          "new": "Use the helper `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"<this skill's folder>\\scripts\\log.ps1\" -LogFile <logfile> -Phase <phase> -Decision <decision> -Why <why> -Evidence <evidence> -Result <result>`. It stamps `ts`, writes the header on first use, strips stray tabs and line breaks, and prefixes any cell starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, or `@` with a single quote. Bind a value that itself starts with `-` with a colon, as in `-Evidence:'-12%'`. Appending a row by hand works too, but write UTF-8 without a byte order mark, and mind those same characters if cells come from generated or user-supplied text."
        },
        {
          "path": "skills/show-me-your-work/SKILL.md",

        },
        {

          "line": 67,
          "old": "spawn a subagent on another vendor's model than the one that did the work.",
          "new": "spawn a reviewer on the `codex` seat (`subagent_type: \"codex:codex-rescue\"`). When that agent is not available, use a Claude model other than the one that did the work, and say that the review stayed within one vendor."

    #!/usr/bin/env node
    // Adversarial tests for scripts/check-dstack.mjs. Copies the repository to a temporary folder,
    // plants one defect per case, and asserts that the checker reports exactly the expected rule.
    // Usage: node tests/adversarial/run.mjs [--source DIR] [--keep] [--case ID]
    // Exit codes: 0 every case behaved as expected, 1 at least one case did not, 2 setup error.
    import { cpSync, existsSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
    import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
    import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
    import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";

    const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
    const flag = (name) => (argv.includes(name) ? argv[argv.indexOf(name) + 1] : undefined);
    const SOURCE = flag("--source") ?? join(here, "..", "..");
    const ONLY = flag("--case");
    const KEEP = argv.includes("--keep");
    const CHECKER = join(SOURCE, "scripts", "check-dstack.mjs");
    const CONFIG = join(SOURCE, "scripts", "check-dstack.config.json");
    if (!existsSync(CHECKER) || !existsSync(CONFIG)) {
      console.error("run.mjs: checker or config not found under " + SOURCE);
      process.exit(2);
    }

    const SKIP = new Set([".git", "node_modules", ".omc"]);
    function freshCopy() {
      const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "dstack-adv-"));
      cpSync(SOURCE, dir, { recursive: true, filter: (src) => !SKIP.has(src.split(/[\\/]/).pop()) });
      return dir;
    }
    function check(root, extra = []) {
      const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [join(root, "scripts", "check-dstack.mjs"), "--root", root, "--json", ...extra], { encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 28 });
      let report;
      try { report = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { report = undefined; }
      return { status: r.status, report, stderr: r.stderr };
    }
    const A = (root, path, text) => appendFileSync(join(root, path), text);
    const W = (root, path, text) => { mkdirSync(dirname(join(root, path)), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(join(root, path), text); };
    const R = (root, path) => readFileSync(join(root, path), "utf8");
    const firstSkill = (root) => readdirSync(join(root, "skills")).filter((n) => n.startsWith("principle-")).sort()[0];
    const B = String.fromCharCode(96);
    const FENCE = B + B + B;

    // Each case: id, what it plants, and expect = [{ rule, path?, count? }]. count defaults to "at least 1".
    // expectClean: the checker must report zero findings.
    const cases = [
      { id: "T00-pristine", plant: () => {}, expectClean: true },
      { id: "T01-pr-upper-md", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/how/SKILL.md", "\nOpen a PR for this change.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PR", path: "skills/how/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T02-pr-lower-md", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/how/SKILL.md", "\nthen open the pr.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PR", path: "skills/how/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T03-gh-pr-in-ps1-fence", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/how/SKILL.md", "\n" + FENCE + "powershell\ngh pr create --fill\n" + FENCE + "\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PR", path: "skills/how/SKILL.md" }] },
      { id: "T04-pull-request-in-code", plant: (r) => W(r, "skills/statechart/scripts/planted.ts", "// see the pull request for details\nexport const x = 1;\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PR", path: "skills/statechart/scripts/planted.ts", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T05-editor-name-md", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nThis works best in " + "Cur" + "sor.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "CURSOR", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T06-editor-rule-path", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nWrite ~/." + "cur" + "sor/rules/x.mdc now.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "CURSOR", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 2 }] },
      { id: "T07-upstream-mode-name", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nSee the " + "pot" + "eto-mode skill.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "IDENTITY", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T08-upstream-plugin-name-json", plant: (r) => W(r, "skills/statechart/scripts/planted.json", "{ \"name\": \"" + "ps" + "tack-tools\" }\n"), expect: [{ rule: "IDENTITY", path: "skills/statechart/scripts/planted.json", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T09-macos-tool", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nRun xc" + "run sim" + "ctl list.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "MACOS", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 2 }] },
      { id: "T10-bash-fence", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\n" + FENCE + "bash\nls\n" + FENCE + "\n"), expect: [{ rule: "SHELL", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T11-harness-term", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nSpawn general" + "Purpose with claude-" + "opus-5-5-xhigh-fast.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "HARNESS", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 2 }] },
      { id: "T12-name-mismatch", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/how/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace(/^name: how$/m, "name: howl")); }, expect: [{ rule: "NAME_MISMATCH", path: "skills/how/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T13-no-description", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/how/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace(/^description:.*$/m, "description:")); }, expect: [{ rule: "NO_DESCRIPTION", path: "skills/how/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T14-skill-folder-without-skill-md", plant: (r) => W(r, "skills/empty-skill/notes.md", "nothing\n"), expect: [{ rule: "SKILL_MISSING", path: "skills/empty-skill/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T15-broken-link", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nSee [the missing file](../no-such-skill/SKILL.md).\n"), expect: [{ rule: "LINK_BROKEN", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T16-route-to-deleted-playbook", plant: (r) => rmSync(join(r, "skills/d-mode/playbooks/autopilot.md")), expect: [{ rule: "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_PLAYBOOK", path: "skills/d-mode/SKILL.md" }, { rule: "COUNT_PLAYBOOKS", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T17-unrouted-playbook", plant: (r) => W(r, "skills/d-mode/playbooks/extra-thing.md", "### Extra thing\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PLAYBOOK_NOT_ROUTED", path: "skills/d-mode/playbooks/extra-thing.md", count: 1 }, { rule: "COUNT_PLAYBOOKS", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T18-reference-to-removed-playbook", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nThen follow " + B + "playbooks/babysit.md" + B + ".\n"), expect: [{ rule: "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_PLAYBOOK", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T19-route-to-missing-skill", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nRun the **nonexistent-thing** skill.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "ROUTE_TO_MISSING_SKILL", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T20-bait-lowercase-in-code", plant: (r) => W(r, "skills/statechart/scripts/bait.ts", "// move the text cursor, then read pr\nexport const pr = 1;\nexport let cursorColumn = pr + 1;\nexport const prs = [pr];\n"), expectClean: true },
      { id: "T21-bait-allowlisted-line", plant: (r) => {
          A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nMove the text " + "cur" + "sor to the end.\nMove the text " + "cur" + "sor again.\n");
          const line = R(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md").split("\n").findIndex((l) => l.includes("to the end.")) + 1;
          const p = "scripts/check-dstack.config.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p));
          c.allowlist.push({ path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", rules: ["CURSOR"], line, reason: "adversarial test: one allowlisted line" });
          W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c, null, 2));
        }, expect: [{ rule: "CURSOR", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }], total: 1 },
      { id: "T22-malformed-plugin-json", plant: (r) => W(r, ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", "{ \"name\": \"dstack\", "), expect: [{ rule: "MANIFEST_INVALID_JSON", path: ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", count: 1 }], validate: true, alsoWhenValidateRuns: "VALIDATE_FAILED" },
      { id: "T23-wrong-plugin-name", plant: (r) => { const p = ".claude-plugin/plugin.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p)); c.name = "other"; W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c)); }, expect: [{ rule: "PLUGIN_NAME", count: 1 }, { rule: "MARKETPLACE_ENTRY", count: 0 }] },
      { id: "T24-wrong-marketplace-source", plant: (r) => { const p = ".claude-plugin/marketplace.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p)); c.plugins[0].source = "./plugin"; W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c)); }, expect: [{ rule: "MARKETPLACE_SOURCE", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T25-ps1-chain-operator", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", "git fetch && git status\r\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_DENYLIST", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T26-ps1-non-ascii", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", "# 한글 주석\r\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_NON_ASCII", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T27-ps1-lf-only", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", "exit 0\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_LF_ONLY", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T28-ps1-out-file", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", "$row | Out-File -FilePath $path -Append\r\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_DENYLIST", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T29-ps1-missing-strict-mode", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace("Set-StrictMode -Version Latest", "")); }, expect: [{ rule: "PS1_REQUIRED_LINE", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T30-md-powershell-fence-chain", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\n" + FENCE + "powershell\ndotnet build && dotnet test\n" + FENCE + "\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_FENCE_DENYLIST", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T31-bom", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/why/SKILL.md"; writeFileSync(join(r, p), Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf]), readFileSync(join(r, p))])); }, expect: [{ rule: "BOM", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }, { rule: "NO_FRONTMATTER", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T32-not-utf8", plant: (r) => writeFileSync(join(r, "skills/why/cp949.md"), Buffer.from([0x23, 0x20, 0xc7, 0xd1, 0xb1, 0xdb, 0x0a])), expect: [{ rule: "NOT_UTF8", path: "skills/why/cp949.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T33-crlf-in-md", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\r\nline with carriage return\r\n"), expect: [{ rule: "CRLF", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T34-deleted-skill", plant: (r) => rmSync(join(r, "skills", firstSkill(r)), { recursive: true }), expect: [{ rule: "COUNT_SKILLS", count: 1 }, { rule: "COUNT_PRINCIPLESKILLS", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T38-hangul-in-skill", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\n\uD55C\uAD6D\uC5B4 \uBB38\uC7A5\n"), expect: [{ rule: "HANGUL_IN_SKILL", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T39-guide-page-not-korean", plant: (r) => W(r, "docs/guide/03-understand.md", "# Understand the code\n\nEnglish only.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "KOREAN_MISSING", path: "docs/guide/03-understand.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T40-guide-page-deleted", plant: (r) => rmSync(join(r, "docs/guide/08-principles.md")), expect: [{ rule: "COUNT_GUIDEPAGES", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T41-language-skill-blocks-auto-load", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/csharp-best-practices/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace(/^paths: (.*)$/m, "paths: $1\ndisable-model-invocation: true")); }, expect: [{ rule: "LANG_SKILL_FRONTMATTER", path: "skills/csharp-best-practices/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T42-language-skill-without-rules-section", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/rust-best-practices/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace(/^## Rules$/m, "## Guidance")); }, expect: [{ rule: "LANG_SKILL_SECTION", path: "skills/rust-best-practices/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T43-language-skill-without-examples", plant: (r) => rmSync(join(r, "skills/python-best-practices/references/patterns.md")), expect: [{ rule: "LANG_SKILL_PATTERNS", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T44-language-skill-not-routed", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/d-mode/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).split("**pml-best-practices**").join("the PML rules")); }, expect: [{ rule: "LANG_SKILL_NOT_ROUTED", path: "skills/d-mode/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T45-plugin-version-pinned", plant: (r) => { const p = ".claude-plugin/plugin.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p)); c.version = "0.1.0"; W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c)); }, expect: [{ rule: "PLUGIN_VERSION_PINNED", path: ".claude-plugin/plugin.json", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T46-korean-residue-in-guide", plant: (r) => A(r, "docs/guide/03-understand.md", "\n풀 리퀘스트를 연다. 커서에서 실행한다.\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PR", path: "docs/guide/03-understand.md", count: 1 }, { rule: "CURSOR", path: "docs/guide/03-understand.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T47-unix-idioms", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nRun " + B + "cat -n notes.txt > /dev" + "/null" + B + " and " + B + "export FOO=1" + B + ", then read " + B + "~" + "/notes" + B + ".\n"), expect: [{ rule: "SHELL", path: "skills/why/SKILL.md", count: 4 }] },
      { id: "T48-bait-legitimate-look-alikes", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/why/SKILL.md", "\nRead " + B + "~/.claude/rules/x.md" + B + ". PowerShell prints " + B + "$($row.Name)" + B + ". It instruments the live process, then you grep the topic and open a file.\n"), expectClean: true },
      { id: "T49-ps1-unbalanced-brace", plant: (r) => A(r, "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", "if ($true) {\r\n"), expect: [{ rule: "PS1_UNBALANCED", path: "skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.ps1", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T50-failure-case-id-removed", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/d-mode/playbooks/commit-and-push.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace("| F05 |", "| FXX |")); }, expect: [{ rule: "REQUIRED_TEXT_MISSING", path: "skills/d-mode/playbooks/commit-and-push.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T51-always-pause-rule-removed", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/d-mode/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace("a push whose verification failed or never ran, ", "")); }, expect: [{ rule: "REQUIRED_TEXT_MISSING", path: "skills/d-mode/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T52-bootstrap-file-removed", plant: (r) => rmSync(join(r, "skills/d-mode/scripts/bootstrap.ts")), expect: [{ rule: "REQUIRED_FILE_MISSING", path: "skills/d-mode/scripts/bootstrap.ts", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T53-deleted-tool-referenced-again", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/d-mode/scripts/package.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p)); c.scripts = { test: "bun test watch-" + "pr" }; W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c, null, 2) + "\n"); }, expect: [{ rule: "FORBIDDEN_TEXT", path: "skills/d-mode/scripts/package.json", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T54-setup-fallback-row-removed", plant: (r) => { const p = "skills/setup-dstack/SKILL.md"; W(r, p, R(r, p).replace("| Missing line |", "| Absent line |")); }, expect: [{ rule: "REQUIRED_TEXT_MISSING", path: "skills/setup-dstack/SKILL.md", count: 1 }] },
      { id: "T35-malformed-config", plant: (r) => W(r, "scripts/check-dstack.config.json", "{ not json"), expectExit: 2 },
      { id: "T36-allowlist-without-reason", plant: (r) => { const p = "scripts/check-dstack.config.json"; const c = JSON.parse(R(r, p)); c.allowlist.push({ path: "README.md", rules: ["*"] }); W(r, p, JSON.stringify(c)); }, expectExit: 2 },
      { id: "T37-unknown-argument", plant: () => {}, args: ["--bogus"], expectExit: 2 },
    ];

    let failed = 0;
    for (const c of cases) {
      if (ONLY && c.id !== ONLY) continue;
      const root = freshCopy();
      const problems = [];
      try {
        c.plant(root);
        const result = check(root, [...(c.validate ? [] : ["--no-validate"]), ...(c.args ?? [])]);
        if (c.expectExit !== undefined) {
          if (result.status !== c.expectExit) problems.push("exit " + result.status + ", expected " + c.expectExit);
        } else if (!result.report) {
          problems.push("checker printed no JSON report (exit " + result.status + "): " + result.stderr.slice(0, 200));
        } else if (c.expectClean) {
          if (result.status !== 0 || result.report.findings.length !== 0) problems.push("expected 0 findings, got " + result.report.findings.length + ": " + JSON.stringify(result.report.findings.slice(0, 3)));
        } else {
          if (result.status !== 1) problems.push("exit " + result.status + ", expected 1");
          for (const e of c.expect) {
            const hits = result.report.findings.filter((f) => f.rule === e.rule && (e.path === undefined || f.path === e.path));
            if (e.count === undefined ? hits.length === 0 : hits.length !== e.count) problems.push(e.rule + (e.path ? " at " + e.path : "") + ": " + hits.length + " findings, expected " + (e.count ?? "at least 1"));
          }
          if (c.total !== undefined && result.report.findings.length !== c.total) problems.push("total findings " + result.report.findings.length + ", expected " + c.total);
          const validateRan = result.report.notes.every((n) => !n.startsWith("SKIP"));
          if (c.alsoWhenValidateRuns && validateRan && !result.report.findings.some((f) => f.rule === c.alsoWhenValidateRuns)) problems.push("claude plugin validate ran but " + c.alsoWhenValidateRuns + " was not reported");
        }
      } catch (error) {
        problems.push("threw: " + error.message);
      } finally {
        if (!KEEP) rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
      }
      if (problems.length === 0) console.log("ok   " + c.id);
      else { failed++; console.log("FAIL " + c.id + (KEEP ? " (" + root + ")" : "") + "\n     " + problems.join("\n     ")); }
    }
    console.log(failed === 0 ? "PASS adversarial: every case behaved as expected" : "FAIL adversarial: " + failed + " case(s)");
    process.exit(failed === 0 ? 0 : 1);
